# PHASE 2 result — tetragonal sweep (batch 1, 2026-09-03)

Spec: `../PHASE2_PLAN.md`; gates `../ANCHORS.md` G3 (enforced per stored sighting), G2b block (accepted Gram modules), KILL CRITERIA (>38 facets = quarantine; congruence violation = (group, b) quarantine). Pipeline: `orbit.py` -> `phase2/metric.py` (integer Gram diag(q^2,q^2,p^2), R^T G R = G asserted) -> `phase2/sweep_voronoi_gram.py` (float proposal, W=2..4) -> `phase2/exact_cell_gram.py` (all-Fraction, G-norm certificate 4 rho^2 <= D^2 asserted; cutoff warm-started from a float-neighbor pre-clip, capped at the module default) -> `canon_code.py`; a second orbit cell is clipped exactly on every candidate with orbit size > 1 (orbit-congruence check). Frozen G1 `spacegroups.json` only. Store seeded with all 102 phase-1 (cubic) types.

**LANGUAGE (G5): every type marked tetragonal below means "not matched against catalog snapshot of 2026-09-03" (= the phase-1 cubic store + this run). NO novelty claim, NO naming. Type-level diligence against Schmitt's tetragonal tables is a LATER step: the machine-readable Schmitt digitization covers the cubic groups only; the P2 screen here is f-vector-level and one-directional (his printed rows reproduce in our pipeline; it says nothing about whether our types appear in his 14 TB).**

## Honest limits (what this sweep did NOT do)

- Orbits: special positions on the per-coordinate {1,2,3,4,6,8,12} grid plus 1/24-grid samples of Wyckoff LINES (dim <= 1) only; plane strata are sampled at the coarse grid only (1/24 plane samples counted and skipped by design); denominators beyond 24 not scanned; general position = 2 rational controls per group (Schmitt's survey covers general position densely).
- Metric: 13 coarse rational b-ratios, 5 Schmitt printed non-grid values (cap 5 of 56 harvested — the rest were NOT run), bisection midpoints to depth 4 (cap 26 evaluations/orbit, 22 cap hits). Irrational transition values (e.g. sqrt 2) are not representable and were bracketed, not hit. Schmitt's 1001-step b-ratio grid is far finer than ours.
- Two exact cells per orbit (representative + one congruence check); the other cells were float-checked for (F, p-vector) only.
- Aut orders are combinatorial map automorphism counts; geometric stabilizer certification is G4/V2, not claimed.
- Type identity relies on canon_code; f/p-vector collisions between distinct codes are not merged.
- Schmitt screen is f-vector-level (his tables print f-vectors, not types) and one-directional; type-level diligence against his tetragonal data is NOT done (digitization covers cubic only).
- Enantiomorphic pairs (76/78, 91/95, 92/96) share Schmitt's printed table; each row was run in both groups with the printed point.
- Budget NOT hit: all five passes completed for all 68 groups; nothing was cut except the explicit caps above.

Run wall time: 961 s (12 worker processes, ordered absorption; phase2_types.json contains no timings except elapsed_s and per-group CPU seconds).
